## Chapter 13｜Market Day Below Ground｜地下市集日

*英文故事正文：819詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The dungeon holds a market that attracts human neighbors and proves the residents can trade peacefully. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

地下城舉辦市集吸引人類鄰居，證明房客能和平交易。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Jin walked it at a normal pace while Mara counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the final door as proof that everything was under control. Jin refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。金以正常速度走一遍，瑪拉數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用最後一扇門證明一切都在掌握中，金拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Dungeon Board before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Jin read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Mara noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到地下城管理會。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。金讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。瑪拉注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "amaze", "anxious", "bud", "charm", "cotton". The next line held "crown", "dairy", "element", "grocery", "holy". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「amaze」、「anxious」、「bud」、「charm」、「cotton」；下一行則有「crown」、「dairy」、「element」、「grocery」、「holy」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Dungeon Board, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Jin asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Mara offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於地下城管理會的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。金請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。瑪拉遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "hourly", "mess", "oak", "ownership", "pineapple". The next line held "representative", "scarf", "sip", "swear", "tag". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「hourly」、「mess」、「oak」、「ownership」、「pineapple」；下一行則有「representative」、「scarf」、「sip」、「swear」、「tag」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Missing Ledger'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈失蹤的帳冊〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 14｜The Missing Ledger｜失蹤的帳冊

*英文故事正文：814詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Jin discovers that a previous landlord erased years of maintenance expenses. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

金發現前任房東刪除了多年的維修費用紀錄。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Mara asked who had the right to make the final decision. Jin could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，瑪拉問誰有權作最後決定。金本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Jin walked it at a normal pace while Mara counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the final door as proof that everything was under control. Jin refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。金以正常速度走一遍，瑪拉數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用最後一扇門證明一切都在掌握中，金拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "breathe", "bunch", "campus", "communicate", "desire". The next line held "electronic", "guidance", "headline", "inferior", "luggage". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「breathe」、「bunch」、「campus」、「communicate」、「desire」；下一行則有「electronic」、「guidance」、「headline」、「inferior」、「luggage」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Dungeon Board before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Jin read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Mara noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到地下城管理會。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。金讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。瑪拉注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "mall", "march", "native", "passport", "saving". The next line held "skinny", "stir", "trunk", "tub", "volleyball". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「mall」、「march」、「native」、「passport」、「saving」；下一行則有「skinny」、「stir」、「trunk」、「tub」、「volleyball」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Dragon in the Basement'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈地下室的龍〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.
C. Jin discovers that a previous landlord erased years of maintenance expenses.

**答案：C。** 核心事件：金發現前任房東刪除了多年的維修費用紀錄。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. Mara
B. The queen of a distant planet.
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：A。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. Hollowkeep
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 15｜A Dragon in the Basement｜地下室的龍

*英文故事正文：821詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A tiny dragon offers to inspect old stonework without waking the unknown creature beyond the door. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

一條小龍願意檢查老舊石造結構，而不驚醒門後未知生物。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Jin sat beside the final door and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Mara brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Jin. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，金坐在最後一扇門旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。瑪拉帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。金並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Mara asked who had the right to make the final decision. Jin could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，瑪拉問誰有權作最後決定。金本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "ash", "award", "boot", "chimney", "colorful". The next line held "historian", "ivory", "joyful", "keyboard", "lung". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「ash」、「award」、「boot」、「chimney」、「colorful」；下一行則有「historian」、「ivory」、「joyful」、「keyboard」、「lung」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Jin walked it at a normal pace while Mara counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the final door as proof that everything was under control. Jin refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。金以正常速度走一遍，瑪拉數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用最後一扇門證明一切都在掌握中，金拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "outer", "palm", "scout", "skate", "snap". The next line held "staff", "subtract", "tow", "yearly", "youngster". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「outer」、「palm」、「scout」、「skate」、「snap」；下一行則有「staff」、「subtract」、「tow」、「yearly」、「youngster」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Second Key'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈第二把鑰匙〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 16｜The Second Key｜第二把鑰匙

*英文故事正文：818詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Mara finds that the final room can only be opened by two separately elected representatives. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

瑪拉發現最後房間只能由兩名分別選出的代表共同開啟。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Mara did not agree with everything Jin said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Jin asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

**繁中**

討論計畫時，瑪拉並不完全同意金的話。爭論始於一個看似微小的細節，卻揭露了兩人對公平的不同理解。有志工提議立刻投票解決，金則要求先聽取風險最高者的意見。回答改變了提案。沒有人喜歡承認自己漏看了什麼，但訂正後房間反而安靜。當分歧能公開存在時，人們更容易做出有用的選擇。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Jin sat beside the final door and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Mara brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Jin. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，金坐在最後一扇門旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。瑪拉帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。金並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "advanced", "advertise", "armed", "bulb", "chop". The next line held "comma", "fuel", "hug", "meadow", "moist". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「advanced」、「advertise」、「armed」、「bulb」、「chop」；下一行則有「comma」、「fuel」、「hug」、「meadow」、「moist」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Mara asked who had the right to make the final decision. Jin could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，瑪拉問誰有權作最後決定。金本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "passion", "quote", "rotten", "separation", "spaghetti". The next line held "spill", "sting", "sum", "thirst", "thread". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「passion」、「quote」、「rotten」、「separation」、「spaghetti」；下一行則有「spill」、「sting」、「sum」、「thirst」、「thread」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Eviction Notice'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈驅逐通知〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. Mara finds that the final room can only be opened by two separately elected representatives.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：瑪拉發現最後房間只能由兩名分別選出的代表共同開啟。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Mara
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Hollowkeep

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 17｜The Eviction Notice｜驅逐通知

*英文故事正文：820詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The board sends an eviction order while a winter storm blocks the upper road. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

管理會送來驅逐令，同時冬季暴風雪封閉上山道路。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was still a distance between understanding a problem and solving it. Jin and Mara walked through Hollowkeep with a fresh drawing, asking the same question in different places. A child noticed a mark that adults had missed. An elder remembered a change made years before. The clues did not fit together at once, but neither did they cancel each other out. Jin marked each source and refused to invent a missing answer. They returned with dirty shoes and a clearer idea of what needed a second visit. Sometimes the small witness was the one who showed the safest way forward.

**繁中**

理解問題與解決問題之間仍有距離。金和瑪拉拿著新畫的圖走過空堡，在不同地方問相同問題。一個孩子注意到大人忽略的記號，一位長者想起多年前的變化。線索沒有立即拼合，卻也不互相抵銷。金標註每項來源，不替空白捏造答案。他們帶著髒鞋子回來，更清楚哪些地方需要再訪。有時指向安全道路的，正是最不起眼的證人。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Mara did not agree with everything Jin said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Jin asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. | L3-0879 | `swell` | v./n. | `swell` | 腫脹 | The next line held "strength", "swell", "ton", "troop", "widen". |
| L3-0915 | `ton` | n. | `ton` | 噸 | The next line held "strength", "swell", "ton", "troop", "widen". |
| L3-0931 | `troop` | n. | `troop` | 一隊 | The next line held "strength", "swell", "ton", "troop", "widen". |

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.
C. The board sends an eviction order while a winter storm blocks the upper road.

**答案：C。** 核心事件：管理會送來驅逐令，同時冬季暴風雪封閉上山道路。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. Mara
B. The queen of a distant planet.
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：A。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. Hollowkeep
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 18｜The Shelter Below｜地下避難所

*英文故事正文：830詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The dungeon shelters stranded travelers and shows the town why tenant safety matters. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

地下城收容受困旅人，讓城鎮明白保障房客安全的重要性。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

'The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.' Mara said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Jin listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Jin could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。」瑪拉讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。金傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。金感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

There was still a distance between understanding a problem and solving it. Jin and Mara walked through Hollowkeep with a fresh drawing, asking the same question in different places. A child noticed a mark that adults had missed. An elder remembered a change made years before. The clues did not fit together at once, but neither did they cancel each other out. Jin marked each source and refused to invent a missing answer. They returned with dirty shoes and a clearer idea of what needed a second visit. Sometimes the small witness was the one who showed the safest way forward.

**繁中**

理解問題與解決問題之間仍有距離。金和瑪拉拿著新畫的圖走過空堡，在不同地方問相同問題。一個孩子注意到大人忽略的記號，一位長者想起多年前的變化。線索沒有立即拼合，卻也不互相抵銷。金標註每項來源，不替空白捏造答案。他們帶著髒鞋子回來，更清楚哪些地方需要再訪。有時指向安全道路的，正是最不起眼的證人。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "almond", "attract", "clinic", "float", "follower". The next line held "inventor", "lawn", "mend", "probable", "product". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「almond」、「attract」、「clinic」、「float」、「follower」；下一行則有「inventor」、「lawn」、「mend」、「probable」、「product」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Mara did not agree with everything Jin said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Jin asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

**繁中**

討論計畫時，瑪拉並不完全同意金的話。爭論始於一個看似微小的細節，卻揭露了兩人對公平的不同理解。有志工提議立刻投票解決，金則要求先聽取風險最高者的意見。回答改變了提案。沒有人喜歡承認自己漏看了什麼，但訂正後房間反而安靜。當分歧能公開存在時，人們更容易做出有用的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "receipt", "robe", "security", "shepherd", "shiny". The next line held "strip", "tank", "tender", "unite", "wagon". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「receipt」、「robe」、「security」、「shepherd」、「shiny」；下一行則有「strip」、「tank」、「tender」、「unite」、「wagon」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'Who Sleeps Behind the Door'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈門後沉睡的是誰〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 19｜Who Sleeps Behind the Door｜門後沉睡的是誰

*英文故事正文：840詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A blueprint identifies the final room as the chamber of an ancient sleeping guardian that keeps the mountain stable. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

藍圖顯示最後房間裡沉睡著維持山脈穩定的古老守護者。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Jin insisted they record both results. Mara watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。金堅持同時記下兩項結果。瑪拉守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。

Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Auction'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈拍賣會〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. A blueprint identifies the final room as the chamber of an ancient sleeping guardian that keeps the mountain stable.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：藍圖顯示最後房間裡沉睡著維持山脈穩定的古老守護者。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Mara
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Hollowkeep

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 20｜The Auction｜拍賣會

*英文故事正文：837詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The board arranges an auction to sell the dungeon as a monster-free military site. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

管理會安排拍賣，打算把地下城賣成沒有怪物的軍事設施。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

A messenger reached Hollowkeep carrying two conflicting reports. Jin read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Mara placed the final door beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Jin replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達空堡。金慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。瑪拉將最後一扇門放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。金回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Jin insisted they record both results. Mara watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。金堅持同時記下兩項結果。瑪拉守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "chill", "comfort", "dash", "fountain", "invitation". The next line held "mathematical", "merry", "opportunity", "outline", "paradise". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「chill」、「comfort」、「dash」、「fountain」、「invitation」；下一行則有「mathematical」、「merry」、「opportunity」、「outline」、「paradise」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

'The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.' Mara said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Jin listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Jin could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。」瑪拉讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。金傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。金感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "persuade", "reduce", "scream", "similarity", "someday". The next line held "stormy", "temper", "trend", "underwear", "vest". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「persuade」、「reduce」、「scream」、「similarity」、「someday」；下一行則有「stormy」、「temper」、「trend」、「underwear」、「vest」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Public Inventory'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈公開清點〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 21｜A Public Inventory｜公開清點

*英文故事正文：826詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Jin and the council publish rent records and repair work, challenging the board's false claims. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

金與議會公開租金及維修紀錄，反駁管理會的虛假指控。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Dungeon Board, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Jin asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Mara offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於地下城管理會的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。金請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。瑪拉遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

A messenger reached Hollowkeep carrying two conflicting reports. Jin read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Mara placed the final door beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Jin replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達空堡。金慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。瑪拉將最後一扇門放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。金回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "apologize", "assist", "bead", "coach", "column". The next line held "dumb", "elect", "feather", "forever", "jealous". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「apologize」、「assist」、「bead」、「coach」、「column」；下一行則有「dumb」、「elect」、「feather」、「forever」、「jealous」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Jin insisted they record both results. Mara watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。金堅持同時記下兩項結果。瑪拉守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "kilometer", "lettuce", "lifetime", "limb", "mankind". The next line held "playful", "rug", "sausage", "scissors", "vocabulary". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「kilometer」、「lettuce」、「lifetime」、「limb」、「mankind」；下一行則有「playful」、「rug」、「sausage」、「scissors」、「vocabulary」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Safe Inspection'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈安全檢查〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. Jin and the council publish rent records and repair work, challenging the board's false claims.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：金與議會公開租金及維修紀錄，反駁管理會的虛假指控。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Mara

**答案：C。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Hollowkeep
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 22｜The Safe Inspection｜安全檢查

*英文故事正文：821詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

An independent team inspects the final door using sensors, without breaking its protective seal. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

獨立團隊用感測器檢查最後房門，不破壞防護封印。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Dungeon Board before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Jin read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Mara noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到地下城管理會。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。金讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。瑪拉注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Dungeon Board, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Jin asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Mara offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於地下城管理會的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。金請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。瑪拉遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。

The next line held "glow", "kingdom", "maid", "occasion", "outdoor". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

| L3-0952 | `vanish` | v. | `vanish` | 消失 | The next line held "scientist", "spite", "squeeze", "sufficient", "vanish". |

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. An independent team inspects the final door using sensors, without breaking its protective seal.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：獨立團隊用感測器檢查最後房門，不破壞防護封印。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Mara
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Hollowkeep

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 23｜The Guardian Speaks｜守護者說話

*英文故事正文：823詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The ancient guardian speaks through the wall and asks not for worship, but for quiet and stable water. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

古老守護者隔牆說話，要求的不是崇拜，而是安靜與穩定水源。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Jin walked it at a normal pace while Mara counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the final door as proof that everything was under control. Jin refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。金以正常速度走一遍，瑪拉數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用最後一扇門證明一切都在掌握中，金拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Dungeon Board before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Jin read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Mara noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到地下城管理會。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。金讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。瑪拉注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "advantage", "brake", "capture", "continent", "dinosaur". The next line held "dragonfly", "drain", "educate", "faith", "gesture". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「advantage」、「brake」、「capture」、「continent」、「dinosaur」；下一行則有「dragonfly」、「drain」、「educate」、「faith」、「gesture」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Dungeon Board, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Jin asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Mara offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於地下城管理會的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。金請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。瑪拉遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "hallway", "immediate", "majority", "nun", "overseas". The next line held "passage", "scholar", "specific", "stiff", "tutor". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「hallway」、「immediate」、「majority」、「nun」、「overseas」；下一行則有「passage」、「scholar」、「specific」、「stiff」、「tutor」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A City Under One Roof'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈同一屋簷下的城市〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 24｜A City Under One Roof｜同一屋簷下的城市

*英文故事正文：817詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A court recognizes the dungeon as a community housing cooperative rather than a monster nest. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

法院認定地下城是社區住宅合作社，而不是怪物巢穴。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Mara asked who had the right to make the final decision. Jin could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，瑪拉問誰有權作最後決定。金本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Jin walked it at a normal pace while Mara counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the final door as proof that everything was under control. Jin refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Jin wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Mara checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Door Stays Closed'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Jin carried the unfinished notes toward Hollowkeep, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，金在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。瑪拉確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈那扇門仍然關著〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。金帶著未完成的筆記走向空堡，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A court recognizes the dungeon as a community housing cooperative rather than a monster nest.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：法院認定地下城是社區住宅合作社，而不是怪物巢穴。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Mara

**答案：C。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Hollowkeep
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 25｜The Door Stays Closed｜那扇門仍然關著

*英文故事正文：819詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：21個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Jin gives the keys to elected tenants and celebrates the first year of honest rent and open accounts. Jin felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Hollowkeep, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Mara stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Jin opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

金把鑰匙交給民選房客代表，慶祝第一年的誠實租金與公開帳目。 群眾尚未完全理解時，金已感到這件新事的分量。在空堡，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。瑪拉站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。金翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Jin sat beside the final door and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Mara brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Jin. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，金坐在最後一扇門旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。瑪拉帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。金並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Mara asked who had the right to make the final decision. Jin could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，瑪拉問誰有權作最後決定。金本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "aboard", "beetle", "bet", "broadcast", "crab". The next line held "destroy", "editor", "enjoyable", "frank", "interrupt". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「aboard」、「beetle」、「bet」、「broadcast」、「crab」；下一行則有「destroy」、「editor」、「enjoyable」、「frank」、「interrupt」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Jin walked it at a normal pace while Mara counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the final door as proof that everything was under control. Jin refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。金以正常速度走一遍，瑪拉數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用最後一扇門證明一切都在掌握中，金拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Mara unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "misery", "motel", "pine", "politics", "regional". The next line held "republic", "romantic", "salary", "studio", "stuff", "victim". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Jin asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，瑪拉攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「misery」、「motel」、「pine」、「politics」、「regional」；下一行則有「republic」、「romantic」、「salary」、「studio」、「stuff」、「victim」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。金詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Mara walked with Jin toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Jin admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Mara answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，瑪拉陪金走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。金坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。瑪拉說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

There was no perfect speech at the end. People in Hollowkeep could question their leaders, change their minds, and share the results without waiting for a chosen hero. Jin remembered the fear of the first day and saw how many ordinary people had helped carry the work. Mara stood nearby, no longer needing to fill every silence. Tomorrow would bring repairs, meals, new arguments, and new mistakes. That was not a weakness of their victory; it was proof that life had continued. They walked home without asking the world to stop changing, ready to make another choice together.

**繁中**

結局沒有完美演說。空堡的人民終於能質疑領袖、改變想法，並分享成果，不必等待命定英雄。金想起第一天的恐懼，也看見有多少普通人共同完成這些工作。瑪拉站在一旁，不再需要填滿每段沉默。明天還會有修理、飯菜、新爭執與新錯誤。這不是勝利的弱點，而是生活仍在繼續的證明。他們一起走回家，不要求世界停止改變，只準備再一起做新的選擇。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. Jin gives the keys to elected tenants and celebrates the first year of honest rent and open accounts.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：金把鑰匙交給民選房客代表，慶祝第一年的誠實租金與公開帳目。

**Q2. Who helped Jin examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Mara
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 瑪拉與金一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Hollowkeep

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是空堡。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The last room must remain closed until every tenant agrees on a safe inspection.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：最後一個房間必須保持關閉，直到所有房客同意安全檢查。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

本書25章故事節點已到達結局。字數、詞條ID、字面命中、段落配對與測驗數量通過程式核對；內容仍為模板輔助學習初稿。劇中詞語紀錄屬字面提及，**不能當成詞性或自然語境教學例句**；跨章重複橋段及翻譯文風尚須實質人工重寫。
